予測

１. 11/16に内閣府から公表される 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.1％（前期比年
率▲0.3％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長になったと推計される。 
２. 高水準の企業収益を背景に設備投資が前期比 1.4％と大幅に増加したが、天候不順の影響
などから民間消費が前期比▲0.3％と減少したこと、原油を中心に輸入が大幅に増加し、
外需が成長率を大きく押し下げたことなどから、小幅ながら 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成
長になったとみられる。 
３. 名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質GDPの伸
びを大きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％、前年比 2.5％と予測する。 
４. 2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時
的な影響が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回
復を妨げる可能性が高い。 
５. 現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまる
と予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-30

2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP 
～前期比▲0.1％（年率▲0.3％）を予測～

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎 
(03)3512-1836  tsaito@nli-research.co.jp

### Page 2

名目 GDPは前期比 0.5％（前期比年率 2.0％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを大
きく上回るだろう。GDPデフレーターは前期比 0.6％（4-6月期：同 1.0％）、前年比 2.5％（4-6月
期：同 2.6％）と予測する。

なお、11/16に内閣府から 2026年 7-9月期の GDP速報が発表される際には、基礎統計の改定や季
節調整のかけ直しなどから、成長率が過去に遡って改定される。当研究所では、2026年 4-6月期の
実質 GDP成長率は、外需、設備投資が上方修正されることから、前期比年率 1.4％から同 2.3％へ
上方修正されると予想している。

2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の一因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。

1 10/30までに公表された経済指標をもとに予測している。今後公表される経済指標の結果によって予測値を修正する可
能性がある。

### Page 3

民間消費は前期比▲0.3％と予測する。 
実質賃金はプラスの伸びが続いているが、物価上昇率の高まりによって実質購買力が押し下げら
れていることに加え、台風や豪雨、長雨などの天候不順が個人消費を下押しした。 
2026年 7-9月期の消費関連指標を確認すると、延べ宿泊者数（うち日本人）は前期比 2.8％（4-6
月期：同▲0.9％）の増加となったが、自動車税の
「環境性能割」の廃止を受けて 4-6月期に前期比
10.7％の高い伸びとなった自動車販売台数が、その
反動で同▲7.5％と落ち込んだほか、外食産業売上
高が前期比▲0.3％（4-6月期：同 0.2％）、小売業
販売額指数が前期比▲2.1％（4-6月期：同 0.6％）
と減少した（いずれもニッセイ基礎研究所による
季節調整値、外食産業売上高、小売業販売額指数
は消費者物価指数で実質化）。

・住宅投資～弱めの動きが続く～

住宅投資は前期比▲0.2％と 2四半期連続の減
少を予測する。 
新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算値）
は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け
込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万戸
から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月
が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸
台半ばで推移している。 
GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響も
あり弱めの動きとなっている。

・民間設備投資～高水準の企業収益を背景に増加～

民間設備投資は前期比 1.4％と 3四半期ぶりの増加を予測する。 
設備投資の一致指標である資本財総供給は 2026年 4-6月期の前期比 4.9％の後、7-9月期も同 4.9％
となった。また、機械投資の先行指標である機械受注（船舶・電力を除く民需）は 2026年 4-6月
期に前期比 0.2％と 3四半期連続で増加した後、7、8月の平均は 4-6月期を 0.4％上回っている。 
日銀短観 2026年 9月調査では、2026年度の設備投資計画（含むソフトウェア・研究開発投資額、
除く土地投資額）が6月調査から 0.5％上方修正され、前年度比9.3％（全規模・全産業）となった。 
設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報

2026年度

・公的固定資本形成～2四半期連続の減少～

公的固定資本形成は、前期比▲2.4％と予測する。 
公共工事の先行指標である公共工事請負金額
は 2026年 7-9月期に前年比▲2.6％と 6四半期ぶ
りに減少した。また、公共工事の進捗を反映する
公共工事出来高（建設総合統計）は、2026年 4-6
月期に前年比 5.2％と 9四半期連続で増加した後、
2026年 7、8月の平均は同 2.4％と伸びが鈍化し
ている。 
2025年度補正予算による押し上げ効果が弱ま
っていることに加え、2026 年度当初予算の公共
事業関係費の伸びが抑えられていることから、弱
めの動きとなっている。

・外需～4四半期ぶりのマイナス寄与～

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.4％（前期比年率▲1.8％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナスを予測する。財貨・
サービスの輸出が前期比 2.0％、財貨・サービスの輸入が同 4.8％となるだろう。代替調達の進展に
伴う原油輸入量の急増を主因として 輸入が高い伸
びとなり、輸出の伸びを上回ったことから、外需は
成長率の押し下げ要因となった。 
2026年 7-9月期の地域別輸出数量指数を季節調整
値（当研究所による試算値）でみると、米国向けが
前期比 0.9％（4-6月期：同 2.8％） 、EU向けが前期
比▲2.4％（4-6月期：同 0.3％） 、アジア向けが前期
比▲0.3％（4-6 月期：同▲0.8％） 、うち中国向けが

Document 122: 261104_daiwa_guc

Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following its results release on 30 October, we raise our 
forecasts and 12M TP for Global Unichip (GUC). The stock more than 
doubled since our last update (Upgrading: unleashed capacity adding to 
growth, 5 August), yet its recent pullback amid increased market volatility 
looks to be unlocking value again for investment, so we reaffirm our Buy (1) 
rating. Fundamentals have unfolded strongly. According to our market 
research, GUC’s key customer in the AI CPU space has pulled forward its 
order trajectory ahead of the previous schedule amid accelerated demand 
for internal and external AI datacentre build-out. This comes on top of its 
initial ramp of another new accelerator project, followed by the next 
generation AI CPU and accelerator ramps for the same customer, with 
further upside from another new AI accelerator customer in the auto space. 
We suggest investors take advantage of the pullback to bargain-hunt.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. In light of its key AI CPU customer 
accelerating its ordering trajectory, together with multiple AI projects 
ramping in the next 1-2 years to fire on all cylinders for growth, we lift our 
2026-28E EPS by 8-50% for GUC. We forecast triple-digit % YoY EPS 
growth over 2Q26-2Q27, before moderating to 50% or so into 2028. While 
such strong growth should help sustain its hefty valuation, further rerating 
may be unlikely as this would depend on GUC’s ability to win more AI 
projects than we expect, and more importantly, whether it is able to secure 
enough capacity from the supply chain to accommodate any incremental 
business amid the constrained supply environment.

Margin-accretive at operating level. On top of the AI CPU demand uplift, 
we expect GUC to have a total of 6 major AI projects in mass-production for 
2027, including 4 CPU and 2 accelerators, all for US-based hyperscalers or 
system vendors mainly focusing on N3 process nodes, helping broaden 
GUC’s AI project base. Although these projects appear margin-dilutive at 
gross level, they should be accretive at the operating level, thanks to 
GUC’s prudent opex management on enhanced operating leverage.

What we recommend: Given our forecast changes, we lift our 12M TP for 
GUC to TWD7,800 (previous: TWD5,125), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 67x (previous: 63x), justified by its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 66% from a PEG perspective. Although further rerating remains 
challenging, we are comfortable with our Buy (1) call due to the recent 
share price pullback unlocking value for investment again. Key downside 
risks to our call would be supply constraints and margin execution.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 23-37% below the consensus 
forecasts as we factor in the supply constraints that will moderate growth.

4 November 2026

Value returns when jitters rise

➢ Lifting forecasts on AI strength and a broadened project base 
➢ Margin dilutive at gross level yet accretive at operating level 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) with a higher 12M TP of TWD7,800

Source: Daiwa forecasts

Share price performance

Source: FactSet, Daiwa forecasts

Company profile 
Founded in 1998, GUC is an IC design service provider focusing on AI/HPC, networking, consumer and 
industrial applications.

### Page 5

5

Global Unichip (3443 TT): 4 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
GUC's Board of Directors (BoD) comprises 8 members, including 4 independent directors (50%
of the board), which is significantly higher than the regulatory requirement of at least 20%. The
Board demonstrates diverse expertise across legal, finance, technology, and business
management. The Chairman and the President are different individuals, ensuring separation of
roles and enhancing governance independence.
Capital management 1
Over the past decade, GUC has maintained a consistently high dividend payout ratio, generally
above 60%, and in most years exceeding 80%. In 2025, the company distributed TWD2,144m in
cash dividends, reflecting its policy of sustaining or increasing dividend amounts over time.
Given GUC’s asset-light business model as an IC design service provider, which does not
require significant capital expenditure for manufacturing facilities, this high payout ratio is
appropriate and aligns with its long-term commitment to shareholder returns.
Related party &
transaction 1
GUC's sole foundry supplier is TSMC, who is also GUC's largest shareholder. As an IC design
service company without onsite production, GUC's relationship with the leading foundry of
advanced process nodes provides it a key competitive advantage, in our view.
S Supply chain management 2
GUC is committed to maintaining long-term partnerships with suppliers and establishing a
sustainable supply chain through measures such as promoting its green action policy and
concept to the entire supply chain, where all its suppliers are required to follow its Responsible
Business Alliance (RBA) regulation. It also periodically conducts supplier audits to ensure the
quality of suppliers.
E Water & wastewater management 1
GUC continues its green living project to reduce water consumption. In 2024, the Company
maintained water-saving measures such as rainwater collection systems, automatic sensor
faucets, dual-flush toilets, and regular inspections of water equipment. These efforts aim to
improve water efficiency and promote conservation across operations.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
As an IC design service provider, GUC does not have in-house manufacturing facilities. It
requires its suppliers to use recycled materials for on-site operation and transportation, and
reduce the amount of expendable resources being used. In order to promote a paperless office,
GUC has established a cloud operating system to reduce the use of paper-based documents.
E GHG emissions 1
GUC adopts energy-saving measures to reduce greenhouse gas emissions and has set annual
electricity conservation targets. In 2024, the Company achieved a 1% reduction in electricity use
and increased green electricity to 39% of total consumption. It also continues to develop low-
power and high-efficiency IP to help minimise environmental impact.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 3

Chroma ATE (2360.TW)
05 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Chroma ATE
Valuation
Our target price of NT$2,900 is based on 40x 2027E P/E, at close to 1x PEG. Chroma shares have re-rated above its historical
range as its growth outlook is now strongly tied to AI infrastructure build out. We estimate 60%+ of its revenue are now AI-
related, with strong growth across product segments including power testers, SLT, metrology, and photonics. Chroma has a
commanding market share in most of these segments and well positioned to capitalized on the global AI expansion.

Risks
Downside risks to achieving our target price include: 1) softer AI/CPO growth expectation due to changes in capex outlook or
production bottleneck; 2) fewer SLT needed as a result of shortened cycle time as GPU yield rate improves; 3) disappointing
metrology shipment due to changes at ramp-up schedule; 4) testing process change leading to loss in market share; 5) FX risks
(50-60% of Chroma’s sales are in US$, 10-20% in Rmb).
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Co-Tech Development Corp.  | Asia Pacific
In-line 3Q26 results
Co-Tech reported 3Q26 results:
• 3Q revenue was NT$3,169mn (+13% q/q, +60% y/y).
• GM was 26.7% (-70bps q/q, +620bps y/y), 20/150bps below MSe/Street,
which we attributed to higher copper pricing and electricity rates (link).
• OP came in at NT$757mn (+11% q/q, +125% y/y), 1% above MSe but 5% below
the Street, for OpM of 23.9% (-50bps q/q, +690bps y/y), 30bps above MSe
but 140bps below the Street.

• As a result, NP was NT$608mn (+12% q/q, +140% y/y), in line with MSe but
4% below the Street, for EPS of NT$2.41.
• Co-Tech’s board also approved up to NT$7bn of CBs to fund the company's
Malaysian expansion. Issue price will be set by auction. Conversion price is
TBD pending regulatory approval.
• Reiterate OW; PT NT$745.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Idea
Muted momentum in near term: Chenbro's 3Q26 earnings fell short of market
expectations by ~30% on lower revenue and margins, owing to customer inventory
digestion, a longer qualification process for its ASIC customer's new product and a
less favorable revenue mix. Looking to 4Q26,  management guided for revenue to
stay roughly flat QoQ (Oct at NT$2,308mn, -7% MoM/+16% YoY) and GM to be
similar to that in 3Q26, as the overhang of new product qualification extends into

4Q26.
2027 still seen as a good year: We believe it continue to grow along with the new
AI server platforms, its customer's share gains and incremental contribution from
new products. We now model for revenue to grow by 35% YoY, GM to be roughly
flat at 29.3% and EPS to rise 39% YoY for 2027.
More diversification into the AI clusters: Chenbro has been leveraging its existing
expertise in mechanical components and customer relationship to diversify from

chassis into other mechanical components in AI applications. It started to see more
meaningful contribution from racks at 5-10% in 2025, with ~10% likely in 2026 and
potentially topping 20% in 2027. For the longer term, it could step into other
mechanical components in the liquid cooling space. Although these efforts could
open up more opportunities for Chenbro to grow, they don't all carry higher-than-
average margins, so potential dilution could be a factor to monitor.

Global footprint continues to expand: To improve its supply chain resilience and
local production capability, Chenbro has several capacity expansion plans globally,
with  Taiwan remaining  the hub for high-end production and R&D. In the pipeline,
Malaysia will likely see new capacity ramping from 4Q26, Vietnam from 2H27 and
US pilot run/MP in 4Q27/2H28. Capex was guided at NT$2.5-3.0bn for 2026.
Downgrade to EW: We believe Chenbro should remain one of the competitive

players in the AI mechanical component space, but near-term hiccups might mute
business momentum. Despite valuation approaching its trough, we see no strong
catalyst in the coming quarters. We thus reduce our rating to EW and lower our PT
by 61% to NT$630 (12x 2027e P/E, implying a PEG of 0.3 vs. the average of 0.5 in
2020-25).
What’s Changed

product should continue to weigh on business momentum and
margins in 4Q26. We believe sentiment should remain lukewarm
in the near term, and thus downgrade the stock.

Results Summary
Off revenue of NT$7,395mn (-5% QoQ/+31% YoY), GM contracted by 6.8ppt QoQ to
25.8%, mainly due to a less-favorable product mix and costs associated with new product
qualification. This was short of MSe at 31.9% and consensus at 30.2%, or by 6.1ppt and
4.3ppt, respectively.
Opex was NT$546mn (opex ratio of 7.4%) vs. MSe of NT$686mn (opex ratio 8.2%) and
consensus of NT$617mn (opex ratio 7.5%), leading to operating profit of NT$1,373mn

(OPM 18.6%). This was 31% lower than MSe at NT$1,996mn (OPM 23.9%) and 26% lower
than consensus at NT$1,854mn (OPM 22.6%).
With a non-op gain of NT$2mn (FX gain of NT$18mn), net profit of NT$1,005mn (EPS NT
$8.21) was 33% lower than MSe at NT$1,500mn (EPS NT$12.25) and 29% below
consensus at NT$1,412mn (EPS NT$11.25).

### Page 6

Idea
6
Valuation Methodology
Our price target of NT$630 (previously NT$1,600) is derived from 12x 2027e P/E (vs.
27x 2027e previously): We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it appropriately captures Chenbro's earnings growth potential. We lower our
target multiple to factor in weaker market sentiment toward the stock on softer
fundamental performance and overall to AI names, as well as muted near-term business
momentum. We believe our target P/E multiple of 12x is justified by an earnings CAGR of

32% seen for 2025-28e, implying a PEG of 0.4. This is lower than the PEG of 0.8 in 2014-
24, when the stock traded at an average P/E of 12x for a 15% earnings CAGR.
Bull case scenario value - NT$1,600 (-29%): We apply a 30x P/E multiple (38x 2027e
previously) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario mainly assumes a better
outlook for AI capex and more project wins from  new customers.
Bear case scenario value - NT$380 (-42%): We apply a 7x P/E multiple (11x 2027e

previously) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario mainly assumes lukewarm
datacenter demand amid softening IT capex budgets and slower progress in its new
product offerings.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  1

2026/11/04 投資速報

電子–半導體 
環球晶(6488.TT) 
短期獲利受火災影響，但供需吃緊及漲價趨勢不變

買進(維持評等) 
出刊緣由：法說會

營收走勢圖

研究員/鄭高輯 OWENCHENG@uni-psg.com

結論: 
環球晶短期獲利表現低於預期，主要原因包括: (1) 義大利 Novara
廠 7 月火災影響、(2)短期產能受限，以及(3)2H26 仍適用舊LTA 價
格 。 不過最壞狀況已過 ， 隨著時序進入4Q26， 預期營收將優於3Q26，
且自 1Q27 起 ， 隨著新產能開出 ， 以及開始適用價格調漲後的新LTA，
獲利表現將明顯轉佳 。 預估2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為17.82 元
(原估 19.49 元)、33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。在投資建議上，儘管
短期營運不如預期 ， 但環球晶股價已大幅修正 ， 且2027 年半導體及
記憶體新 Fab 產能開出的趨勢明確(13 座->23 座)， 因此矽晶圓供需
轉緊且價格上漲的趨勢不變， 故維持買進建議 ， 然考量近期AI 供應
鏈出貨不順的雜音較多 ，漲價概念股評價受壓抑 ， 故將PBR 由 6X 調
整至 5X (PBR 歷史區間 2~6X，漲價循環合理評價應在中上緣 4~6X
之間)，將目標價由 1350 元下調至 1170 元。

重點摘要:

1.3Q26 財報表現不佳，主要受義大利 Novara 廠火災影響:  
3Q26 營收-2.3%QoQ(+2.6%YoY)，若排除 7 月義大利 Novara 廠火災
之影響，營收原本可以成長雙位數%QoQ。在毛利率方面，儘管3Q26
折舊費用略較前季下降 ， 但因Novara 火災導致部分高毛利率的 8 吋
SOI 基板出貨中斷 ， 導致毛利率較前季下滑0.6pt 至 20%， 不過在費
用控管下，OPM 較前季回升 0.4pt 至 9.8%。單季稅後 EPS 3.13 元，
低於原預期的 3.64 元 ， 且明顯低於前季的7.9 元 ， 差異主要係2Q26
認列較多 WAF 金融評價利益。

2.美光支付 5 億美元並非是預付款，而是認購 CB:  
今年 7 月環球晶與美光宣布簽訂 10 年期 LTA，美光將支付環球晶 5
億美元，然而，並非以預付款形式支付，而是環球晶發行上限 5 億
美元的可轉債，由美光擔任唯一應募人。研究員認為，客戶支付預
付款綁定產能的強制力相對較強 (預付款在出貨後會轉營收且不退
還)，然而，CB 存在股本稀釋的問題(推估約 3%上下)，相對較不受
歡迎。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3.2027 年起開始適用新 LTA 價格，ASP 將改善:  
在價格趨勢方面，公司對於價格調整將採取逐案談判 (Case-by-
case)，依據產品規格、供需狀況、合約到期時間與個別客戶情況進
行協議，非全產品線統一調漲特定百分比。公司認為目前市場環境
日益支持價格回歸合理水準，且已有多筆新 LTA 接近洽談完成，預
計自 2027 年起整體 ASP 將受惠於新 LTA 與產品組合優化而有所改
善。

3.4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26，但受限產能，且價格仍沿用舊合約:  
展望 4Q26，義大利 Novara 廠已正式啟動部分復工，且整體客戶需
求動能依然非常強勁 ， 甚至有客戶願意提前適用新價格以爭取產能 ，
但受限於產能不足 ， 且4Q26 絕大多數價格仍適用舊合約 ， 因此預期
4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26， 但仍無法達成雙位數%成長 。 不過自1Q27 起，
隨著新產能開出以及 ASP 提升，營運動能將轉佳。研究員預估2026
年營收-1.0%YoY，稅後EPS 17.82 元(原估 19.49 元，下修主要是因
為火災)。

4.2027 年營運將明顯好轉，大致維持獲利預估:  
展望 2027 年 ， 根據研調統計 ， 開始營運投片的新Fab 數量將自 2026
年的 13 座大幅增加至 2027 年的 23 座 ， 且成熟製程庫存健康化 ，電
源管理、車用與工業恢復，公司預期 2027 年矽晶圓供需環境將比
2026 年更吃緊。就環球晶而言，自 1Q27 起隨著新產能開出，且開
始適用調漲後的新 LTA 價格，將有助於營收及獲利轉強。此外，隨
著 AI 資料中心發展 ，SiC 市場正步入新成長週期 ，12 吋 SOI 晶圓也
受惠矽光子強勁動能推升需求，密蘇里州廠是目前美國唯一具備12
吋 SOI 製造能力的基地，產能持續爬升，客戶意向及預付款已可涵
蓋擬新增產能的 90%以上。基於以上，環球晶將步入產業上升循環，
預估 2027 年營收+33.8%YoY，稅後 EPS 33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言 
© 2010 統 一 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 。 本 公 司 提 供 之 報 告 內 容 係 根 據 本 公
司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26 년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G 는 +11.5%, ASP 는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G 는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP 는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L 는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

SK하이닉스 (000660)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로 
차별화

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic 에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Document 256: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면  이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

TUẦN 02-06/11 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh 
khoản mở rộng

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Diễn biến phân hóa và bi ến động mạnh có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ vọng sẽ tiếp tục 
hướng lên thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh khoản mở 
rộng. Thiết bị dụng cụ Y tế, Tiện ích khí và Bảo hiểm là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần qua. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị 
-870 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã MBB, VIC và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
ở mức 504 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng -2,010 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua 
và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -10,888 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 1,208 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -4,070 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam có tuần giảm điểm thứ 2 liên tiếp khi VN-Index lùi về kiểm định vùng hỗ trợ 
quanh 1.725 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm giảm hơn 30 điểm trước khi thu hẹp 
đà giảm nhờ lực cầu ở nhóm chứng khoán. Áp lực bán tập trung ở nhóm vốn hóa lớn, đặc 
biệt là bộ đôi Vingroup (VIC, VHM) cùng một số cổ phiếu ngân hàng và công nghệ, khiến 
chỉ số thiếu lực đỡ. Giao dịch của khối ngoại diễn biến trái chiều và giằng co — có phiên 
mua ròng trở lại ở một số mã, nhưng vẫn xen kẽ bán ròng ở nhóm trụ như VHM, VCB, cho 
thấy dòng vốn ngoại chưa xác lập xu hướng rõ ràng. Bối cảnh bên ngoài kém thuận lợi khi 
nhóm cổ phiếu công nghệ tại Mỹ bị bán tháo, kéo theo các chỉ số chính toàn cầu đồng 
loạt lao dốc. Mặc dù vậy, kỳ vọng vào động thái mở kênh hoán đổi ngoại tệ của NHNN để 
hỗ trợ thanh khoản, hạ lãi suất đã giúp tâm lý nhà đầu tư trong nước ổn định và không 
xuất hiện áp lực bán tháo.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến spinning thân nhỏ với bóng trên/dưới khá dài, 
cùng thanh khoản gia tăng, thể hiện sự giằng co mạnh nhưng về tổng thể lại cho thấy diễn 
biến đã cân bằng trở lại sau tuần giảm điểm trước đó. Trên khung ngày, mặc dù trải qua 
các phiên biến động tăng/giảm mạnh đan xen nhưng việc cho phản ứng bật tăng tích cực 
tại quanh đường biên dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn cho thấy tương quan 
vẫn đang nghiêng về chiều hướng tăng. Diễn biến phân hóa và biến động mạnh có thể còn 
tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ tiếp tục hướng lên thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Hỗ trợ xa: 1660 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Document 320: CoreWeave陷入困境：股价高点已蒸发65%，盘后强劲财报也难平息市场忧虑

Source PDF: all report pdf/CoreWeave陷入困境：股价高点已蒸发65%，盘后强劲财报也难平息市场忧虑.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

CoreWeave 将于周⼆收盘后公布第三季度业绩。但投资者的关注焦点并不在财报数字本
⾝，⽽在于：上周期中选举结果令多个州的数据中⼼建设前景蒙上阴影，管理层将如何回
应这⼀变局。
摘要：
市场对 AI 基础设施前景的担忧，已⾮⼀份靓丽财报所能消解。
云计算公司 CoreWeave 将于周⼆收盘后公布第三季度业绩。但投资者的关注焦点并不在财报数
字本⾝，⽽在于：上周期中选举结果令多个州的数据中⼼建设前景蒙上阴影，管理层将如何回
应这⼀变局。
CoreWeave 股价上周重挫 15% ，⾃六个⽉前⾼点⾄今已累计下跌 65% ，期权市场数据显⽰，财
报公布后该股波幅预计约为 16% 。
数据中⼼建设阻⼒、科技巨头资本开⽀收紧以及公司⾃⾝债务压⼒，三重⻛险正在同步累积。
微软据报道正收紧对部分项⽬的监管，并退出其认为⻛险过⾼的项⽬；与此同时，英伟达⾸席
执⾏官⻩仁勋深陷个⼈丑闻，其领导层地位⾯临不确定性，⽽英伟达既是 CoreWeave 的关键供
应商，也是其投资⽅。
选举结果重击新云，监管环境趋严

上周期中选举结果成为压垮市场情绪的直接导⽕索。多地选举结果显⽰，⼤量选⺠明确反对在
其社区附近建设数据中⼼，这使得倾向于对 AI 基础设施项⽬持更保守态度的⺠主党籍州级和地⽅
政府的影响⼒⼤幅上升。
以德克萨斯州为例，候任州⻓ Gina Hinojosa 已公开呼吁⽴即暂停数据中⼼项⽬审批。
CoreWeave 随即致函 Hinojosa ，表达寻求合作的意愿。
选举结果对不同类型科技公司的影响出现明显分化。上周微软、亚⻢逊和 Alphabet 等超⼤规模
云服务商（ hyperscaler ）股价在选举后反弹，⽽以 CoreWeave 为代表的 " 新云计算 " （ neocloud ）
公司股价则⼤幅下挫， Nebius 和 IREN 同样跌势显著。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
这⼀分化折射出两类公司的本质差异：对超⼤规模云服务商⽽⾔， AI 资本开⽀放缓或意味着⾃由
现⾦流改善；⽽对于依赖 AI ⾼速增⻓来⽀撑商业模式的新云计算公司， AI ⽀出和建设放缓则直接
威胁其核⼼业务。
50 Park Investments ⾸席执⾏官 Adam Sarhan 表⽰：" 美国选⺠⽤选票表明，他们不希望数据中
⼼建在附近。这将使新云计算公司的处境更为艰难，因为它们没有其他收⼊来源可以弥补 AI 业
务的潜在损失。 "
债务模式埋下脆弱隐患，债券价格跌⼊困境区间
CoreWeave 本⾝的财务结构使其在此轮⻛险中尤为脆弱。该公司通过借贷融资建设数据中⼼，
这意味着必须维持持续的⾼速增⻓才能覆盖债务成本。
选举结束后， CoreWeave 债券价格随即下挫，部分债券收益率已突破 15% ，进⼊市场通常视为

困境债务的区间。
根据彭博汇编的分析师预期数据，CoreWeave 第三季度营收增速预计超过 160% ，但净亏损规
模将较上年同期⼤幅扩⼤。公司预计⾄ 2029 年前仍将持续亏损，且此后每年收⼊增速均呈下滑
态势。
持续亏损意味着 CoreWeave 按多数估值指标衡量仍显昂贵，尽管⽬前市销率约为 1 倍 —— 接近历
史最低⽔平，仅为公司 2025 年 3 ⽉上市以来平均市销率的四分之⼀左右。
Founder ETFs 投资组合经理 Michael Monaghan 表⽰："AI ⽀出前景的重要性远超财报本⾝，因
为市场担⼼期中选举是否意味着 AI 基础设施建设进⼊减速周期。 "
本轮 AI 财报季情绪持续承压
CoreWeave ⾯临的困境并⾮孤例，⽽是本轮 AI 财报季整体情绪恶化的缩影。
本轮财报季已出现多起⾼知名度的 " 利好出尽 " ⾏情：⾕歌、 Meta 、亚⻢逊和英特尔相继遭遇财报
后抛售，就连微软和闪迪的强劲业绩也未能阻⽌股价下跌。
更深层的问题在于投资者对 AI 资本回报的质疑持续发酵。在缺乏更明显投资回报的情况下，各⽅

对科技公司⼤规模 AI 资本开⽀的可持续性愈发审慎。
Sarhan 直指核⼼⽭盾：" 如果 AI 收⼊枯竭，这些公司的公允价值⼏乎肯定低于当前股价。在 AI 以
外，它们的增⻓将从何⽽来？如果⽆法依靠增⻓来偿还债务，⼜将如何维持偿债能⼒？ "
他也承认，从短期技术⾯看，新云计算股已存在超卖迹象， " 但⾯对如此⾼度的不确定性，很难
想象它们能重回过去的估值⽔平 " 。

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>

Date:    6 hours ago
Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 4 Nov 2026
View in browser

I. Why Wall Street Is Cheering the Blue Sweep
Ahead of Tuesday's midterm elections, we warned that a Democratic
sweep of Congress could deliver a cold shower to Wall Street. It still could,
but it didn't do so today. Democrats captured both chambers of Congress,
flipping seven Republican-held Senate seats, including historic victories in
Texas, Kansas, and South Carolina. Financial markets put a positive spin
on the blue wave.
Oil prices fell on hopes that the US and Iran are more likely now to resolve
their war diplomatically. If so, then the outlook for inflation will improve. So
the Fed might not have to raise the federal funds rate as much. The

### Page 2

federal deficit might not widen further if Democrats refuse to increase
defense spending, while President Donald Trump vetoes their attempts to
provide Medicare for all. If Democrats slow the AI buildout, hyperscalers'
profits and free cash flow will increase. Fewer AI-related bond issues
would also help keep a lid on bond yields.
Initially, Nasdaq-100 futures plunged as much as 2.4% overnight, while
S&P 500 futures fell 1.4%. But the selloff reversed after Trump struck a

surprisingly conciliatory tone, congratulating Democrats and saying he
was willing to work with them.
That is when the cheer on Wall Street started. The Dow surged 3.0%
today, the strongest post-midterm rally since 1982. The S&P 500 jumped
1.6%, its best session in three months. The Nasdaq-100 advanced 0.9%.
The relief rally extended beyond equities. The 10-year Treasury yield fell
to 4.90%, back in what we have called the "old normal" range of
4.00%-5.00% (chart).

The dollar index (DXY) fell more than 1.5%, its biggest decline since
September. Gold rallied back above $4,000 an ounce. It might be finally
bottoming.

### Page 3

de-escalation in the Middle East
Brent crude plunged 8.7% to $94.62 a barrel amid hopes of Middle East
de-escalation. The nearby futures price of gasoline plunged to $2.73
(chart).
Investors may be warming up to the idea that some constraints on the AI
buildout could be healthy, as we have been arguing recently. Democratic
victories in key data center states, including Texas and Georgia, raise the
prospect of tighter construction restrictions amid concerns about electricity

and water consumption.
Hyperscalers rallied today, with Amazon and Microsoft gaining more than
6%, as capital discipline could ease concerns about AI investment returns
and improve free cash flow (chart). AI infrastructure providers struggled,

### Page 4

however. The PHLX Semiconductor Index fell 3.1% amid fears of weaker
demand from a slower buildout. We may be entering an AI Goldilocks
zone, with enough restraint to take some air out of the bubble without
derailing the boom.
II. The US Economy Continues To Perform Well
The economy and, therefore, earnings remain fundamentally resilient,
which continues to support the bull market. With the midterms behind us
and political uncertainty receding, investors can refocus on these strong

fundamentals. Here are the highlights of today's upbeat economic
indicators:
(1) ISM Services PMI. The ISM-NM PMI edged down modestly to 54.5 in
October, marking its 28th consecutive month of expansion (chart).
Underlying demand remained solid, with new orders and business activity
both comfortably above the 50.0 expansion threshold. Encouragingly, the
employment index rose from 50.1 to 51.4, marking its second consecutive
month of expansion.

### Page 5

Inflation is still a concern. The ISM prices-paid indexes remained elevated
in October (chart).
(2) Labor market. The average of the ISM employment indexes rose to
51.9 in October, its highest reading in roughly three years (chart). ADP
reported an 86,000 increase in private payrolls in October, beating
consensus expectations of 67,000.

### Page 6

(3) Trade balance. The latest trade data point to resilient US and global
economic growth. Merchandise imports rose 2.4% m/m in September to a
record $3.44 trillion annualized, while exports increased 2.5% m/m to
$2.09 trillion. The merchandise trade deficit widened to $1.35 trillion
annualized (chart).
Nominal and real US merchandise exports plus imports rose 21.1% y/y
and 10.1%, respectively, in September (chart). That suggests global trade
remains firmly in expansion territory and is consistent with strong global

economic growth.

### Page 7

Selected technology imports reached a record $61.3 billion in September,
reflecting the AI buildout’s heavy reliance on imported semiconductors,
servers, and other hardware (chart).

Turning point?
• We expect the unemployment rate to edge
down to 5.5% for the September quarter.
• Job numbers have been gaining momentum
in recent months, and now look to be enough
to outstrip population growth. However, the
participation rate will be the deciding factor.
• Despite cost-of-living pressures on workers,
the existing slack in the labour market is likely
to keep wage growth subdued.
• Our forecasts are a little more positive than

what the RBNZ assumed in their September
policy statement. Assuming they deliver on
an OCR hike this afternoon, the labour market
surveys could have an important bearing on
whether they follow up with another hike in
December.

The New Zealand economy has returned to growth over
the last year, despite a number of headwinds. That’s been
accompanied by some modest growth in jobs, though
to date it hasn’t been enough to prevent a further rise

in the unemployment rate. However, we think that next
Wednesday’s labour market reports could show the jobs
market reaching a turning point.
We expect the unemployment rate to ease from 5.6%
to 5.5% in the September quarter, which would mark
the first fall in almost five years. That’s not a firmly held
view, given the usual margin of uncertainty around this

Westpac New Zealand Economics
measure. But we’re now seeing consistent evidence
that jobs growth is outstripping the modest pace of
population growth. We also note that the previous result
was 5.56% in unrounded terms, so it wouldn’t take much
to tip the rounded rate down to 5.5% this time.
On the whole, our forecasts are a little stronger than
what the RBNZ assumed in its September Monetary
Policy Statement. While our unemployment rate forecast

is identical, we’re more positive on both employment
and labour force participation. Meanwhile, our pick of a
0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index is slightly ahead of the
RBNZ’s estimate of 0.4%.
Assuming that the RBNZ delivers the OCR hike that we
expect this afternoon, we think they will be noncommittal
about the chances of another hike at the 9 December
Monetary Policy Statement. In that respect, the labour
market surveys could be important in setting market

expectations for the December review, as there aren’t
that many major data releases between now and then.
Forecast details.
We expect the Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS)
to show a 0.5% rise in employment for the September
quarter. That’s broadly in line with the Monthly
Employment Indicator (MEI), with the September release
this morning showing a third straight month of growth
in filled jobs. We note that employment growth in the
HLFS has actually outstripped the MEI for the last nine

quarters, but it’s not clear why this has been the case and
we’re wary of extrapolating it too far into the next quarter.

Employment growth of around 0.5% would be more than
enough to absorb the modest 0.3% rise in the working-
age population, and on its own would suggest a fall in

the unemployment rate. But it also comes down to the
rate of labour force participation, which we don’t really
have much guidance on. The participation rate has been
choppy over recent quarters, and in the June quarter
we saw an unusually large 0.3pt rise to 70.7%; we’re
assuming some payback this time with a more modest
0.1pt rise to 70.8%.
We’ll also be keeping an eye on the broader indicators of
slack in the labour market, although we don’t formally use

these in our forecasts. The June quarter saw a sharp rise
in the underutilisation rate from 12.9% to 13.8%. However,
we think there’s some residual seasonality in this series,
relating to one component in particular: people who are
available for work but not actively looking. In that case,
we could see a substantial pullback in the underutilisation
measure this time.
Previously we’ve characterised the labour market as a
“low-fire, low-hire” environment, with much of the rise in

unemployment reflecting the difficulty of breaking into
the workforce, rather than job losses. There are some
signs of a thaw emerging, with job vacancies picking up
from their lows over the last year and business surveys
pointing to a lift in hiring intentions. But we still expect
that it will be a long road to recovery – our current
forecasts see the unemployment rate at 5% by the end of
next year.
While cost-of-living pressures are again rearing their

head as a major concern for workers, the existing degree
of slack in the labour market will continue to make it
difficult for them to push for bigger pay increases. We
expect a 0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index (LCI) for
the September quarter, which would nudge the annual
growth rate up slightly to 2.1%. That said, we don’t have a
lot to go on for this forecast. The MEI includes a measure
of median weekly earnings, which has shown no sign of
acceleration so far – but this is only a very loose indicator

of wage pressures, as it will also reflect any changes in
the composition of jobs and pay rates.

Document 388: NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-10)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-10).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

#labour #newzealand
NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications
By Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist, Westpac New Zealand
13:16 November 04 2026 Share
The Q3 labour market statistics were weaker than expected. We discuss potential
implications for the RBNZ noting that a December hike remains more likely than not at this
point.
Q3 Labour market data: potential RBNZ implications
The Q3 labour market data were weaker than RBNZ and our expectations.

### Page 2

Today's labour market data seem unequivocally weaker than expected by either us or the RBNZ. While
likely not a gamechanger for the cycle overall, it will add ammunition to the arguments of the MPC
doves that a pause in interest rate rises will make sense soon - perhaps in December or in the New
Year. It’s likely the case that a bit much was priced into December, so the change in market
probabilities in the wake of today’s data makes sense - although my base case remains that a
December rate rise remains more likely than not.
The message from the RBNZ in the October meeting though was to be careful about pricing in "done
deals" and that the outlook will be data-dependent. As we noted at the time, alongside measures of
inflation expectations, the key domestic data to watch ahead of the RBNZ’s 9 December meeting will
be indicators of what is happening to excess capacity (the output gap) as this will be a key determinant
of the Bank’s assessment of the outlook for inflation over the coming year.
It’s useful to remember in the RBNZ’s forecasting framework that GDP figures are the main
determinant of excess capacity in the economy (i.e., the output gap). The Q2 GDP data told us that the
output gap probably narrowed a bit through Q2, mainly reflecting historical revisions. In light of today’s
labour market numbers, the forecast for growth in Q3 may have some downside risk - but there are
conflicting indicators there, so it’s not that clear. However, it’s probably hard to argue that the level of
excess capacity reduced further in Q3 given today’s data. 
In the eyes of dovish MPC members, the rise in the unemployment rate and other measures important
for the RBNZ's labour market dashboard likely push in the direction of having greater comfort with the
medium-term inflation outlook. The vacancy to unemployment ratio increased a bit, labour constraints
eased a bit, and the unemployment rate has increased. Hence from a labour market perspective it
could be argued that policy could have more time all else equal. The outcome of the election, with the
possibility of a new RBNZ mandate, could increase the prominence of these factors - although this
won’t likely be an explicit factor in the RBNZ’s thinking in December even though it may be in the minds
of individual MPC members.
It could be the case that the argument for increases in the OCR track through 2027 could be
undermined with this data. It’s important to note (probably self-evidently) that there are other data that
will also be relevant. Hence the risk of a pause earlier in the New Year has probably increased with this
data, all else equal. I don't think our forecast peak OCR is really at issue here, but the time taken to get
there could be more flexible. Takes along the lines of "3% is the terminal rate" are very premature and
misplaced, in our view.

A December hike remains more likely than not, but there will be a clear debate to be had between the
MPC hawks and doves.
The forecast peak OCR is likely less impacted than the time to reach that peak. Further pauses in the
tightening cycle could come earlier than currently forecast. 
The outlook for the exchange rate is especially clouded by this data should it take a longer time for
NZ rates to move closer to global norms. 
Watch for updated RBNZ commentary on 10 and 19 November. The Governor’s observation that it
would have been useful to update markets ahead of the October meeting could imply a greater
willingness to comment in the coming weeks.

### Page 3

The outlook for the exchange rate is undermined by this data. I feel very happy about our year-end
target of 79c for the NZD/AUD rate with the risk that a recovery in the NZD takes longer than currently
assumed.
Looking ahead, there are some opportunities for the RBNZ to update markets on their views. Top of
mind is Paul Conway's 10 November speech to the UBS Australasia Conference. The Governor is also
speaking to the Institute of Directors on 19 November. If the RBNZ has something to say, then it might
happen in conjunction with these events (the 10 November speech is titled “How is the NZ economy
tracking”, so it will be difficult for Conway to avoid saying something about how he is interpreting
today’s data). We also note the Governor's comments in the October MPR that it could have been
useful to have been more flexible about updating markets on emerging views of new economic data.
This might increase the likelihood that these events could be used to provide guidance on the RBNZ’s
views of this data (and other emerging events), especially if it feels that the market’s interpretation is at
odds with its own.

Document 428: Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

Threats to European diesel imports: risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased 
risk of price rises

In Europe, the possibility of a US ban on diesel exports has heightened concerns about price trends for this widely used 
fuel1, which has already risen significantly since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran. It has become a major political issue 
on both sides of the Atlantic in the run-up to decisive elections.

A particularly tight diesel market due to a shortage of buffer stocks 
Following the outbreak of the conflict, diesel prices did indeed see some of the sharpest rises on the oil market due to particularly 
tight market conditions. Since the end of February, whilst the price of a barrel of crude has risen by 46%, diesel prices have soared 
by 100% in Europe and by 76% in the United States.

The factors alleviating pressure on the refined products market, and more specifically the diesel market, were more limited than in 
the case of crude oil. Shipments passing through the Strait of Hormuz (around 17% of maritime diesel trade prior to the conf lict) 
have fallen by around 75%. Added to this is the halt in Russian exports due to war damage, which is depriving the diesel market of 
around 12% of its maritime exports. Furthermore, unlike with crude oil, China has not acted as a stabilising  force in the diesel 
market. On the contrary, the energy crisis has led to a halt in Chinese exports of refined products (including diesel) to the  rest of 
Asia from April onwards. The resumption of Chinese exports over the last two months remains fragile, given, in particular, that 
diesel stocks are at their lowest level in 15 months.

The United States has eased these tensions in the diesel market by increasing its exports of refined products (up 25% on average 
in the first half of 2026 compared with the average over the last five years). This rise in exports has required high refinery utilisation 
rate (close to maximum capacity) and, to a lesser extent, a reduction in stocks (-11% compared with the average over the last five 
years as at mid -September for middle distillates 2). All these factors have contributed to tightening the US domestic market and 
pushed retail diesel prices to record levels. Road diesel prices (US average) have risen by 68% since the end of February and by 
74% year-on-year.

A structurally high level of vulnerability in Europe… 
Against this market backdrop, characterised by mounting tensions and very limited room for manoeuvre , Europe would be 
particularly hard hit by a reduction in – or even a complete halt to – US exports. According to Eurostat, imports accounted for 46% 
of European demand for diesel in the first half of 2026, with 40% of these imports coming from the United States. Europe, which

1 Diesel accounts for 17% of the primary energy mix in the EU and 7.3% in the United States. 
2 A category of refined petroleum products comprising mainly diesel and aviation fuel.

### Page 2

has lost a great deal of production capacity in recent years, is operating its refineries at near -maximum capacity. Its room for 
manoeuvre is therefore limited.

… But the risk of a shortage has been ruled out in the short term 
The risk of a shortage, however, appears limited in the short term. The situation regarding European stocks is not comfortable, but 
it is not alarming. Commercial diesel stocks in the ARA region 3 are currently at historically low levels (around 12 million barrels), 
23% below their level at the end of September 2025. As for emergency stocks (EU Emergency Stocks), these are, according to the 
European Commission, at a sufficient level. Eurostat estim ates this figure at 270 million barrels in June 2026 (stable from a year 
earlier), representing approximately 60 days’ worth of EU consumption. Europe imports around 0.9 mb/d of diesel from the United 
States, and emergency stocks would therefore cover around 60 days’ consumption at 4.5 mb/d.

The rise in prices could be significant 
Whatever the outcome of the conflict, diesel prices are likely to continue to rise in the short term. According to our base -case 
scenario, crude oil prices are expected to remain very high in Q4 2026 due to ongoing tensions in the Persian Gulf, whilst a return 
to normal levels of diesel exports from Russia and the Middle East appears unlikely by that time.

Among the factors likely to cause future tensions, the start of the maintenance season in Europe could affect production leve ls, 
whilst demand for diesel in the United States is typically high in the autumn (agricultural harvests and pre -winter storage). I n a 
scenario where tensions remain low, the diesel refining margin (crack spread) could rise by 5% in Q4 compared with its level at the 
end of September 2026, before gradually falling back during 2027. An intermediate scenario (a 10% rise in the crack spread in Q4 
2026) would result in moderate constraints on US diesel exports and a drawdown of European stocks. Finally, an adverse scenario 
(a 35% rise in the crack spread in Q4 2026) would imply severe restrictions on US diesel exports and a delayed European response.

What impact will this have on inflation in the euro area? 
According to our estimates, the rise in diesel prices would exacerbate the expected peak in inflation in the euro area without altering 
its timing. In our baseline scenario for September 2026 (see our recently published EcoPerspective), headline inflation would peak 
at 4.1% y/y in January 2027 before gradually falling back towards 2% by the end of the year. The two least adverse scenarios 
would add only a limited amount to the peak: +0.1 pp in the first scenario (+5% for the crack spread in Q4 compared with the end 
of Q3) and +0.2 pp in the intermediate scenario (+10%), with inflation reaching 4.2% and 4.3% respectively in January 2027. 
However, there is an upside risk in the second scenario linked to the uncertain extent of the spillov er effects. In both cases, the 
main impact would be felt through fuel prices (which quickly reflect rising market prices) and would fade over the course of 2027. 
In the case of the adverse scenario (+35%), the additional inflation would be close to 0.6 pp by early 2027, pushing inflation above 
4.6%. A shock of this magnitude would, in fact, reduce businesses’ ability to absorb rising costs, which would lead to price increases 
across a broader range of goods (food, industrial goods). Subsequently, the effect would only fade gradually.

Pascal Devaux 
Economist, Middle East, Western Balkans – Energy 
pascal.devaux@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe, Eurozone, International Trade 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

3 Amsterdam-Rotterdam-Antwerp, Europe’s main hub for the storage of raw materials.

《消費量能未見失速訊號》
• 9月卡片消費YoY回升至+6.2，
顯示量能尚未失速
1
資產 4Q26 看法 投資展望
核心消費 中立 本行維持產業上下半年營收成長48：52的溫和成長預期，消費產業在旺
季前呈現「量能可望持平、獲利品質分化」，同時零售商備貨行為與終
端消費需求仍支持到貨量的基本盤，但消費者實際支出意願可能受到油
價／利率上升抑制，戰術性減碼受油價與信心承壓較重的旅遊休閒類股
4Q26預計呈「量能可望維持、獲利偏弱」格局
資料來源：美國銀行2026/10/15，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28。

• 消費量能未見失速訊號 P.1
• 「K型」分化持續收斂 P.2
• 4Q26傳統銷售旺季Bottom-Up P.3
• 核心消費三個次產業需求驅動各異 P.4
• 成本與定價動能 P.5
消費產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/29
BofA信用卡數據9月卡片消費YoY回升、零售銷售續增(惟
剔除汽油站僅+0.2%),延續8月自7月疲弱中的反彈,為本行
對量販/民生通路保持加碼提供量能基準。
總卡片消費YoY成長率於6-8月連續降溫(6月6.3%→7月
5.0%→8月4.5%),剔除油品後同步走緩
(5.6%→4.3%→3.7%)，主因6月世界盃與線上促銷時序錯
位帶來的高基期效應消退。9月YoY回升至6.2%、剔除油品
為5.1%(BofA指出皆為近四年第三強),惟季調後MoM僅
+0.2%(7月-0.2%、8月+0.9%),剔除油品後幾乎持平,顯示
YoY反彈部分來自油價推升,月度動能趨於溫和。投資意涵：
量能未見明顯失速，不支持對消費產業全面削減曝險,宜改
採結構性次產業輪動

《「K型」分化持續收斂，中低所得消費有收入基礎》
低所得家戶的消費與稅後薪資成長自 7月起皆居三分位
（Tercile）組之首 ，高所得 （不含前5%）已被中 、低所
得超越。三分位組中，低所得家戶的消費與薪資成長皆最
強；前5%仍是消費成長最快的族群 ，但已較高點放緩 。
各組消費成長均高於稅後薪資成長約1個百分點（pp）。

9月各所得階層信用消費成長率 vs. 稅後薪資成長率
中低所得消費有收入基礎 ，但低所得薪資成長由 5.2%降
至4.5%；前5%消費成長持續高於薪資成長 ，依賴財富效
果。

《4Q26傳統銷售旺季Bottom-Up需求強弱度分析》
NRF已於10月15日發布2026年假期旺季(11-12月)專屬銷
售預測：零售銷售預估年增4.2%至1.013兆美元(2025年
為+3.1%、9,720億美元；不含汽車經銷商、加油站與餐
飲)，成長率高於去年,但NRF亦提醒,通膨、油價上升與家
庭支出壓力對不同所得族群的影響將有差異。此前3月18
日發布的2026全年預測(+4.4%至5.6兆美元)則早於中東
衝突推高油價與聯準會重啟升息。
資料來源：密西根大學2026/10月初值，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28
資料來源：NRF、零售商財報及GFMS數據綜合評分 2026/10，中國信託商業
銀行整理，2026/10/28

《核心消費三個次產業的需求驅動並不相同》
核心消費三個次產業的需求驅動並不相同：量販受惠於價
值定位與低中所得客流，民生必需品的成長主要來自銷量
與產品組合而非漲價，餐飲則因外食價格通膨與客群分化
而最為脆弱。

量販：居家食品價格溫和（9月 YoY +1.8%、MoM
−0.1%），外食價格則上行（+3.5%），兩者價差擴大，
從價格面支持在家用餐與量販通路的相對優勢，這是trade-

down的價格面訊號。Costco 9月可比銷售的總數字
（+11.4%）與剔除油價及匯率後的數字（+7.6%）相差約
3.8個百分點，觀察時應以剔除後的數字為準。Walmart的
Q3同店銷售指引區間（+3.0%至+3.75%）可作為檢驗自
發性需求的基準。

### Page 5

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
重點摘要
零售客流量表
5
民生必需品：P&G有機成長 +4.0%中，銷量貢獻 +2.4%，
價格貢獻有限；引用的 NielsenIQ資料也顯示家庭與個人
護理類 +4.3%、包裝食品 −0.9%。成長主要來自銷量與
組合而非漲價，且品類間分化明顯，這是本行僅給予中性
評等的主因。
餐飲：2Q26同店銷售呈現明顯分化 ，本行預估客流領先
的連鎖與咖啡系統維持正成長 ，倚賴折價促銷的速食與披
薩鏈許多為負成長 。在外食價格比居家食品高出約 1.7個
百分點的環境下 ，客流與價格的取捨將是 4Q26餐飲評等
的主要變數。
《成本與定價動能》
9月CPI顯示，整體CPI月增 +0.6%、年增 +3.7%，核心
CPI年增 +2.6%；能源年增 +20.4%、汽油年增 +34.2%，

是整體通膨上行的主因。對核心消費而言，關鍵在於食品
與能源的分離：居家食品月增 −0.1%、年增 +1.8%，外
食年增 +3.5%。
對毛利的含意：能源成本上升而零售端食品價格溫和 ，代
表核心消費業者較難以轉嫁成本 ，壓力更可能先反映在毛
利率而非銷售額 。P&G在指引中揭露本財年約 14億美元
稅後成本逆風（含其他逆風合計約每股0.79美元，約拖累
核心EPS成長11%），為公司自行揭露數字 ，本行未重新
計算 。Kimberly-Clark則預期 2026年有機銷售成長約落
後 所 屬 品 類 加 權 成 長100bp ， 顯示在品類成長僅約
+1.7% 的環境下價格與組合的操作空間有限 。成長動能
偏向「量」而非「價」；此結構有利於營收穩定，但對毛
利率的支撐較弱。

Document 492: イスラエル総選挙の結果と新政権の行方 ～アイゼンコット政権樹立には未だ険しい道のり～

Source PDF: all report pdf/イスラエル総選挙の結果と新政権の行方 ～アイゼンコット政権樹立には未だ険しい道のり～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Global Trends

2026 年 11 月 9 日

1/3 
イスラエル総選挙の結果と新政権の行方 
～アイゼンコット政権樹立には未だ険しい道のり～ 
 経済調査部 シニア・フェロー  嶌峰 義清 
（℡：050-5474-7489）

（要旨） 
○ 10 月 27 日に実施されたイスラエル総選挙は、ネタニヤフ首相が率いる与党リクードが第一
党の座を保ったものの、連立与党で過半数を得るには至らなかった。 
○ 各党への意見聴取を経て、ヘルツォグ大統領は第二党となった野党ヤシャルのアイゼンコッ
ト党首を組閣候補者に指名した。ただし、首相の座に就くには①アラブ系政党の協力を得る
か、②与党内でネタニヤフ首相と距離を置く超正統派政党の協力を得られるか、③ネタニヤ
フ首相抜きの挙国一致型連立を実現できるかなどの選択が必要で、その壁は大きい。 
○ トランプ大統領がイスラエルとの距離を置く姿勢を見せるなど、イスラエルを取り巻く国際
情勢は厳しい。これが、欧米との連携の重要性を認識しているアイゼンコット氏の連立模索
にどのような影響を与えるか、今後の中東情勢を占ううえでも注目される。

1. 与党リクードは第一党を死守も、組閣指名はアイゼンコット氏に 
10月 27日投開票のイスラエル総選挙は、11 月4 日に正式結果が確定した。ネタニヤフ首相が
党首を務める与党リクードは32議席から5 議席減らして27 議席となったが、第1党の座は死守
した。もっとも、与党陣営は計53議席、アイゼンコット元軍参謀総長の野党陣営は計48議席、
アラブ系2 党は19議席（ジョイント・リスト11、ラアム8）で、いずれも過半数の61議席に届
かなかった（図表1）。 
イスラエルでは、選挙結果を受けて大統領が各党の意見聴取を行い、組閣を行う候補を指名す
る。指名された候補者は（通常は第一党党首） 、各党と連立を模索し、過半数の得票を得れば新た
な首相の座に就くことになる。ヘルツォグ大統領は各党への意見聴取を行い、11 月5日、組閣を
担う候補の推薦状況を公表した。その結果は、アイゼンコット氏が67 人、ネタニヤフ氏が46 人
と、議席獲得数では第二党となるヤシャルのアイゼンコット党首が1位となった。過半数を超え
る67人の推薦をアイゼンコット氏が獲得した最大の理由は、アラブ系2党（19 議席）がそろって
同氏を推薦したことである。アラブ系政党はネタニヤフ氏や極右政党の政策を差別的とみなし、
「政権交代の実現」を最優先に行動したと考えられる。

### Page 2

2/3 
注目すべきは、長年ネタニヤフ氏を支えてきた超正統派の「統一トーラー・ユダヤ教連合」
（UTJ、7議席）が推薦を見送った点だ。これは「ネタニヤフ氏を推さない」という意思表示でも
あり、与党陣営内部の亀裂が露呈した。ヘルツォグ大統領自身も「結果は明確に見えても、組閣
能力のある人物を判断することはまったく明確ではない」と、陣営内の相互拒否を指摘している
（大統領府） 。かくして大統領は11月 8日、第1党党首ではなく第2党党首のアイゼンコット氏
に組閣を指示した。イスラエル政治の慣例「最大会派の党首」が覆された瞬間である

2. アイゼンコット氏が首相の座に就く可能性 
アイゼンコット氏には28日間の組閣期限が与えられる。最大 14日間の延長も可能だが、仮に
不成立となると別の議員に4 週間の組閣権限の機会が移り、それでも失敗すれば再選挙の可能性
が高まる。新政権の発足までは現職のネタニヤフ氏が暫定首相にとどまる。つまり、交渉が長引
けば長引くほど、暫定ではあれ首相席にとどまり続けるネタニヤフ氏に時間が味方する可能性が
ある。 
アイゼンコット氏が組閣に成功し、新首相の座に就くには大きく三つの経路がある。 
第一は、アラブ系政党からの閣外協力を得て少数与党政権を樹立する道である。自身の陣営48
議席に、アラブ系19 議席の棄権・容認を加えれば、信任投票は乗り切れる。ただし、同氏は「シ
オニスト政党に基づく政権」を掲げてアラブ系の入閣に条件を課しており、また野党陣営内にも
アラブ系との協力に反発する政党がある。連立の枠外からの協力という「曖昧な形」になるだけ
に、政権の安定性を欠く。 
第二は、超正統派政党（UTJ、シャス）との連立である。野党陣営48 議席にUTJ7議席とシャス
6 議席を加えれば、ちょうど61 議席となる。UTJはネタニヤフ氏への推薦を見送っただけに、現実
化する可能性は否定できないが、最大の障壁は徴兵問題である。アイゼンコット氏は国民皆兵を
政党名 立ち位置 議席数
リクード（Likud） 右派 27
オツマ・イェフディット（ユダヤの力）（Otzma Yehudit） 極右 7
統一トーラー・ユダヤ教（UTJ） 超正統派 7
宗教シオニズム（Religious Zionism） 宗教右派 6
シャス（Shas） 超正統派 6
合計 53
ヤシャル（Yashaer） 中道 21
ベヤハド（Beyachad） 中道 12
民主党（The Democrats） 中道左派 8
イスラエル・ベイテヌ（わが家イスラエル）（Yisrael Beitenu） 世俗右派 7
共同リスト（The Joint List） アラブ系・左派連合 11
ラアム（統一アラブリスト）（Ra'am） アラブ系・イスラム系 8
合計 67
注：「連立与党」「野党」の枠組みは、2026年選挙前のもの。

（図表1）イスラエル国会（クネセト）の党派別議席数

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
掲げ、超正統派の徴兵免除の縮小に正面から取り組む姿勢を示しているが、これは超正統派の存
在基盤に触れる問題だ。今回のUTJの推薦見送りは交渉の扉が開いていることの表れとも読める
が、条件面での隔たりは大きい。 
第三は、リクードの切り崩し、あるいは挙国一致政権である。アイゼンコット氏は8日の組閣
着手に当たり、ネタニヤフ氏が「国を歴史的な危機に陥れた」と警告する一方、同氏と連携する
勢力にも協力を呼び掛け、自身の下での入閣にリクードや極右政党の参加も排除しない意向を示
した。ただし「ネタニヤフ氏抜きのリクード」をどれだけの議員が受け入れるかは不明で、第1
党を割く切り崩しは容易ではない。

3. アイゼンコット首相誕生なら、イスラエルを取り巻く環境に改善の可能性も見えるが 
アイゼンコット氏が組閣に着手した11月 8 日の演説は、 「軍事作戦の目標は達成されず、国際
関係は壊滅的な危機にある」 「このまま進めば後戻りできない地点に達するおそれがある」という
危機感に貫かれていた。政権の枠組みがどうあれ、「10・7（ハマスによるイスラエルへのテロに
より多数の死傷者が発生した事件） 」後 3年を経た国の疲弊が政権交代の機運を後押ししているこ
とは間違いない。他方、ネタニヤフ氏は確定結果の直後、アラブ系政党が「票を買収した」と主
張して受け入れを拒否し、巻き返しの構えを見せている。 
今後の焦点は三つに絞られる。第一に、アラブ系の閣外協力を容認する少数政権という「イス
ラエルでは前例の少ない」形が、野党陣営内の反発を乗り越えられるか。第二に、超正統派が
「反ネタニヤフ」の便宜と引き換えに、徴兵問題でどこまで歩み寄れるか。第三に、期限内の合
意が不可能と判断された場合、別の候補に機会が移った際にネタニヤフ氏が再び組閣の座を取り
戻すのか。交渉の行方は12 月上旬までに焦点化しよう。いずれにせよ、暫定政権が長期化するほ
ど政策の停滞コストは積み上がる。 
米国のトランプ大統領は、イスラエルに対する年間約40 億ドル規模の軍事・資金援助を2026
年末で打ち切ると表明するなど、イスラエルと距離を置くかのような発言も見せている。これに
対し、イスラエルのヘルツォグ大統領は「青天の霹靂」と述べ、強い衝撃を受けていることを示
した。中間選挙の敗北を受け、米政権内部でもその敗因をイランへの軍事行動だとする見方が隠
されなくなっている。イスラエルの危機感は非常に強いと考えられるが、それが組閣への動きに
どのように影響するか、今後の中東情勢を見極めるうえでも注目される。

以 上

設備投資（日銀短観）
日銀短観9月調査によると、26年度の設備投資は全産業で前年比＋9.8％と前回6月調査と同水準が見込

Document 617: 動見觀瞻：通膨壓抑科技股走勢，韓股出現絕佳買點

Source PDF: all report pdf/動見觀瞻：通膨壓抑科技股走勢，韓股出現絕佳買點.pdf

Date: 2026-10-30

### Page 1

台北市大安區仁愛路四段 169 號

通膨壓抑科技股走勢，韓股出現絕佳買點

目前韓國股市具備不錯的風險報酬比 ， 建議投資人可以定期定額分批布局 ， 若風
險承受度較高 ， 可於市場震盪或拉回時逢低加碼 ， 以掌握企業獲利及產業成長的
雙重機會。 
出刊日：2026/10/30 
276 期

### Page 2

2026

02-2771-6699 
 www.fubon.com 
台北市大安區仁愛路四段 169 號 
動見觀瞻

本期內容： 
1. 美國通膨再次升
溫 
2. 利率走高，科技
股走勢壓抑 
3. 展望樂觀，估值
「極度便宜」

重點摘要 
■ 美國通膨受能源、住房等價格上漲帶
動回升 ， 但市場認為這屬階段性壓力 ，
未明顯上修終端利率預期。目前仍以
2027 年底前累計升息 3 碼為主情境 ，
下次升息預期落在 12 月 FOMC 會議 。

■ 通膨升溫推升市場利率，導致成長股
估值遭下修，科技與半導體族群出現
震盪。韓國 KOSPI 指數 10 月跌幅達
18.8%， 台股 、 美股及費半亦同步回檔 ，
但產業基本面尚未出現明顯轉弱。

■ 長鑫存儲擴產消息壓抑記憶體產業股
價，但高盛研究顯示其未來產能僅能
滿足一半的中國內部需求，故國際記
憶體的供需狀態難以因此改變。韓國
KOSPI 預期本益比僅 4.9 倍，落入過
去五年負 3 倍標準差以下，具備長期
投資價值。

韓國 KOSPI 指數近期出現大幅修正

資料來源：Bloomberg, 2026/10/16

通膨壓抑科技股走勢，韓股出現絕佳買點

### Page 3

2026

02-2771-6699 
 www.fubon.com 
台北市大安區仁愛路四段 169 號

▋ 美國通膨再次升溫 
美國 9 月 CPI 年增率由 8 月 3.4%升至 3.7% 
(高於預期的 3.6%)，核心 CPI 亦由 8 月
2.4%升至 2.6%。 近期通膨再度走揚的主因
並非需求全面過熱 ， 而是能源價格大幅上漲
所帶動，9 月汽油價格月增 9.1%，約貢獻
整體 CPI 增幅的一半。此外，住房、汽車及
服飾價格亦持續上升 ， 使通膨壓力出現由能
源向部分服務與商品擴散的現象。

圖一: 美國 9 月 CPI 再次升溫

資料來源：Bloomberg/北富銀整理, 2026/10/16

然而 ， 市場對聯準會政策路徑的看法並未出
現明顯鷹派轉向，因核心通膨雖有回升但仍
遠低於 2022 年的高點。美國就業市場顯露
降溫跡象，9 月非農新增就業僅 2.9 萬人，
顯示經濟活動正在放緩 。 因此 ， 利率期貨市
場雖持續反映聯準會仍有進一步緊縮空間 ，
但截至 2027 年底 ， 整體隱含情境仍以累計
約 3 碼(75bps)升息為主，並未因本次通膨
數據而顯著上修終端利率預期 。 目前市場普
遍認為下一次升息更可能落在12 月 FOMC
會議 ， 而非立即於近期會議採取行動 ， 反映
投資人將通膨回升視為以能源驅動為主 ， 而
非全面性再通膨的開始。 
圖二: 美債期貨預期 28 年前累計升息 3 碼

資料來源：Bloomberg/北富銀整理, 2026/10/16

▋ 利率走高，科技股走勢壓抑 
雖然市場認為聯準會升息步調將偏向循序
漸進 ， 但較高的利率環境已開始壓抑成長型
資產表現 。 對科技股而言 ， 其評價多建立在
未來數年的獲利成長 ， 當折現率上升時 ， 未
來現金流現值將被下修 ， 市場因而調降類股
估值 ， 導致近期科技與半導體族群普遍承壓 。

圖三: 韓國 KOSPI 指數修正比例遠超其他

資料來源：Bloomberg/北富銀整理, 2026/10/16

韓國股市受衝擊最為明顯，10 月單月跌幅
達 18.8%，且相較年中高點已回吐約七成漲
幅 。 由於韓國市場高度集中於半導體與科技
權值股 ， 在利率上行與風險偏好下降的環境
下，修正幅度相對較大。相較之下，台灣加
權指數 、 費城半導體指數及美國標普500 指

### Page 4

2026

02-2771-6699 
 www.fubon.com 
台北市大安區仁愛路四段 169 號

數雖也面臨估值收縮壓力 ， 但因企業獲利與
AI 資本支出需求仍具支撐，回檔幅度較韓
國股市溫和。

▋ 展望樂觀，估值「極度便宜」 
儘管近期市場擔憂中國記憶體大廠長鑫 存
儲(CXMT)擴產可能衝擊DRAM 產業供需 ，
但從產業基本面來看，隨AI 伺服器、HBM
及雲端資料中心需求持續成長 ， 全球DRAM
市場仍處供給吃緊狀態 。 根據高盛最新研究 ，
長鑫未來產能即使持續擴張 ， 其規模仍僅約
相當於中國本土需求的一半 ， 主要扮演進口
替代角色 ， 對中國以外市場的供需格局影響
有限 。 因此 ， 全球記憶體產業供不應求的核
心邏輯並未改變 ， 產業獲利展望仍具支撐 。

圖四: 長鑫擴產仍無法滿足中國內部需求

資料來源：Goldman Sachs/北富銀整理, 2026/10/9

與此同時， 近期市場對韓國股市的悲觀情緒
已大幅反應在估值上。目前韓國 KOSPI 指
數未來 12 個月預期本益比僅約 4.9 倍，不
僅遠低於過去五年平均 9.7 倍水準 ， 更跌破
負 3 倍標準差，屬歷史極為罕見的估值位
置 。 由於韓國市場權重高度集中於記憶體 、
半導體及科技龍頭 ， 在產業需求維持成長但
股價大幅修正的情況下 ， 獲利與評價面已出
現明顯背離 。 若未來通膨與利率預期逐步穩
定，當前 KOSPI 所隱含的悲觀假設有望獲
得緩解， 「極度便宜」 的估值水準也將提供
投資人較佳的風險報酬比。

圖五: 韓國 KOSPI 指數估值「極度便宜」

資料來源：Bloomberg/北富銀整理, 2026/10/16

▋ 結論 
科技產業長期展望仍維持樂觀。AI 基礎建
設 、 高效運算與記憶體需求成長趨勢並未改
變 ， 但短期市場仍可能受到通膨回升 、 利率
上行及估值修正影響而出現震盪回檔 。 因此 ，
投資策略維持動見觀瞻 274 期的「科技＋
能源」配置架構。

區域配置方面 ， 美股 、 費半及台股雖出現修
正 ， 但估值仍處中性水平 ，反觀韓國股市未
來 12 個月預期本益比已降至過去五年平均
負 3 倍標準差以下的極端低估值區域。    
考量記憶體基本面依舊強勁 ， 目前韓國股市
或具備較佳的風險報酬比 ， 建議投資人可以
定期定額分批布局 ， 若風險承受度較高 ， 可
於市場震盪或拉回時逢低加碼 ， 以掌握企業
獲利及產業成長的雙重機會。

5347.TT 世界 
 VSMC 進度優於預期

   公司簡介 世界先進於專注於 8 吋晶圓代工，為成熟特殊製程晶圓代工廠，目前擁有五座八吋晶 圓廠，分別位於台灣與新加坡，12 吋晶圓廠將採合資模式並於新加坡建置。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 150 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，195 
【大盤指數/股價】39702 / 120

➢ 3Q26 符合財測，獲利高於預期 
世界先進 3Q26 營收 157.02 億元(QoQ+10.2%：YoY+27.2%)，出貨量
751K，季增 5%。產能利用率約 90%，毛利率 33.7% (QoQ+1.3ppts，
產能利用率＆產品組合改善)， 營利率22.1%(QoQ+1.7ppts)， 業外收入
2.91 億元主要為利息收入，淨利 30.99 億元 EPS 1.65 元(QoQ+4.2%：
YoY+82%)高於我們預估的 1.45 元。 
➢ 4Q26 營運維持高檔 
世界先進目前訂單能見度長達 4 個月，拉貨動能主要來自於 PMIC 及
mix-singal，目前 PMIC 營收占比已達 82%，預估 晶圓出貨量季 增
0%~2%，ASP 季增 3%~5% (價格調整)，GM 34%~36%，投顧預估世
界先進 4Q26 營收為 168.2 億元(QoQ+7.1%)， 毛利率35.5%季增約 1.9
個百分點，淨利 35.29 億元(QoQ+13.9%)，EPS 1.88 元，整體產能利
用率上升到到 90%以上(細線寬產能全數滿載)。 
➢ VSMC 有機會提前轉虧為盈 
VSMC 產能 44K(interposer、mixed-signal、 電源管理產品 、analog IC。
終端應用包括：高速運算 HPC、汽車電子、工業應用、消費性電子、行
動裝置。相關技術授權與技術轉移來自 TSMC)。將於 1Q27 開始進入量
產並逐季擴充產能，季度產能利用率維持在 90%以上，2027 年至 2028
年為 ramp-up 階段，於 2029 年 H1 建置完成 ， 由於interposer 產品佔
比超越一半，目前 VSMC 損平點將低於 30K 月產能。 
➢ 展望樂觀，調升至買進評等 
世界先進目前訂單能見度拉長到 4 個月，而VSMC 在客戶技轉、投資規
模縮小狀況下將有機會提前轉虧為盈，2026 年 AI 營收占比已經超越兩
位數，2027 年將進一步增加，且價格也可反應成本的增加，投顧預估世
界先進 2026 年/2027 年營收分別為 592.98 億元/935 億元，EPS 分別
為 6.31 元/6.79 元優異水準，過去 PE 區間為 7~30 倍，調升至買進評
等，目標價 150 元(2027F PE 22X EPS)。

### Page 2

個股報告

3Q26 符合財測，獲利高於預期 
世界先進 3Q26 營收 157.02 億元(QoQ+10.2%：YoY+27.2%)，出貨量 751K，季增
5%。產能利用率約 90%，毛利率 33.7% (QoQ+1.3ppts，產能利用率＆產品組合改
善)， 營利率22.1% (QoQ+1.7ppts)， 業外收入2.91 億元主要為利息收入 ，淨利 30.99
億元 EPS 1.65 元(QoQ+4.2%：YoY+82%)高於我們預估的 1.45 元。製程別：0.18µm 
and Advanced 佔營收 62%(季增 2 個百分點)；0.25µm、0.35µm、0.5µm 分別佔 14%、
12%、12%。平臺別：電源管理佔 82%(季增 5 個百分點)，大尺寸面板驅動 IC 10%、
小尺寸面板驅動 IC 3%、其他 5%。 
4Q26 營運維持高檔 
世界先進目前訂單能見度長達 4 個月，拉貨動能主要來自於 PMIC 及 mix-singal，目
前 PMIC 營收占比已達 82%，預估晶圓出貨量季增 0~2%(受瓶頸設備產能限制，整體
產 出 僅 能 小 幅 提 升) ，ASP 季 增 3%~5%( 反 映 成 本 轉 嫁 與 有 利 產 品 組 合) ，GM 
34%~36%，投顧預估世界先進 4Q26 營收為 168.2 億元(QoQ+7.1%)， 毛利率35.5%
季增約 1.9 個百分點，淨利 35.29 億元(QoQ+13.9%)，EPS 1.88 元，整體產能利用率
上升到到 90%以上(細線寬產能全數滿載)。 
VSMC 有機會提前轉虧為盈 
VSMC 產能 44K(interposer、mixed-signal、電源管理產品、analog IC。終端應用包
括：高速運算 HPC、汽車電子、工業應用、消費性電子、行動裝置。相關技術授權與
技術轉移來自 TSMC)。將於 1Q27 開始進入量產並逐季擴充產能，季度產能利用率維
持在 90%以上，2027 年至 2028 年為 ramp-up 階段，於 2029 年 H1 建置完成，由
於 interposer 產品佔比超越一半，目前 VSMC 損平點將低於 30K 月產能。AI 需求：
管理層認為 AI 算力需求趨勢確定且不可逆，美國期中選舉後的市場調整有助 AI 轉向
合理長期成長，而非過度集中資源的狂熱形態。 
成熟製程競爭：中國大陸同業擴張成熟製程，但因地緣政治與應用需求差異，直接競
爭威脅不大；部分國際大廠陸續關閉 6 吋、8 吋廠，8 吋成熟產能成長性趨緩，公司持
續關注並尋求機會。 
市佔率：PMIC 與 Discrete 在 foundry 中約居前三大至前五大；Discrete 全球市佔
約 10%~15%，在 foundry 端約 40%~50%。 
資本支出維持 600~700 億元 
2026 年全年資本支出預計落在新臺幣 600 億至 700 億元之間(其中近 90%用於 VSMC
新加坡廠建置與設備，其餘約 10%用於 8 吋廠例行維修及優化)；2026 年全年折舊費
用約為新臺幣 91 億元(年增 6%)。8 吋廠產能調整：2026 年持續汰換並升級部分現有
產能至細線寬製程，2026 年全年 8 吋晶圓產能預估約為 330.6 萬片(年減約 4%)；第
四季產能預估季增 1,000 片至 28 萬片 8 吋晶圓。終端市場狀況：車用市場持平穩定；
工業用工控市場穩定成長；消費性電子與 PC/手機需求相對疲弱，但對整體 90%以上
的產能利用率無顯著負面影響。 
展望樂觀，調升至買進評等 
世界先進目前訂單能見度拉長到 4 個月，而 VSMC 在客戶技轉、投資規模縮小狀況
下將有機會提前轉虧為盈，2026 年 AI 營收占比已經超越兩位數，2027 年將進一步
增加，且價格也可反應成本的增加，投顧預估世界先進 2026 年/2027 年營收分別為
592.98 億元/935 億元，EPS 分別為 6.31 元/6.79 元優異水準，過去 PE 區間為 7~30
倍，調升至買進評等，目標價為 150 元(2027F PE 22X EPS)。

预览内容
在这次中期选举之前，不管是国内还是国外，很多卖⽅研报都提到，如果⺠主党获胜，会
对市场造成⼀定冲击。但从上周四的市场表现，以及上周五⼒度⾮常弱的反弹来看，市场
明显没有完全准备好。
我觉得这⾥⾯有两个原因。
第⼀个，虽然⼤家都觉得⺠主党会赢，但此前普遍预期的是 51 个、 52 个参议院席位，最多
看到 54 个。最后，它的确拿到了 54 个席位，赢得⽐很多⼈原先预期的要多。
第⼆个，我觉得更关键的是，它赢得⾮常⼲脆。在美国不同地区，投给⺠主党的⽐例都有
上升。有些地⽅，与其说是⺠主党赢下了这场选举，不如说是⼤家不愿意把票投给选票上
的共和党候选⼈。
这就要求市场重新思考未来的很多假设，其中⼀个很重要的问题，就是数据中⼼。
这⼏天，我看到⼀些⾃⼰⽐较能接受的说法。⽐如，已经审批通过的数据中⼼，要⻢上全
部撤回、不准修建，我觉得会存在很多困难，法律上也不⼀定完全允许 ……
点击此处或下⽅图⽚解锁完整版内容，继续阅读。
